    47	const BOOK_UNAVAILABLE = Object.freeze({ status: "UNAVAILABLE", reason: BOOK_UNAVAILABLE_REASON });
    48	// The trader-facing reason: what this feed is, in the words the chart shows.
    49	const HEALTH_REASON_BASE = "Polled spot candles · no tape · no order book";
    50	const HEALTH_REASON_VARIANTS = Object.freeze({
    51	  RATE_LIMITED: "rate limited",
    52	  POLL_FAILED: "poll failed",
    53	  CATCHING_UP: "catching up",
    54	  SLOW_POLLING: "slow polling (weight budget)",
    55	});
    56	const SERVED_EVENT_TYPES = new Set([EVENT_TYPES.KLINE]);
    57	const TRANSPORT_FAILURE_REASONS = new Set([
    58	  "NETWORK_ERROR", "TIMEOUT", "HTTP_ERROR", "INVALID_RESPONSE", "MALFORMED_RESPONSE_BODY", "RESPONSE_TOO_LARGE", "FETCH_UNAVAILABLE",
    59	]);
    60	const PERPETUAL_STATUS = Object.freeze({ public: "DISCONNECTED", market: "DISCONNECTED" });
    61	
    62	function binanceTransportFromEnv(env = process.env) {
    63	  const raw = env.TT_BINANCE_TRANSPORT;
    64	  if (raw === undefined || raw.trim() === "") return TRANSPORTS.WS;
    65	  const value = raw.trim();
    66	  if (!Object.values(TRANSPORTS).includes(value)) {
    67	    throw new Error(`TT_BINANCE_TRANSPORT must be ${Object.values(TRANSPORTS).join(" or ")}`);
    68	  }
    69	  return value;
    70	}
    71	
    72	function assertConsumerId(consumerId) {
    73	  if (typeof consumerId !== "string" || consumerId.length === 0) {
    74	    throw new Error("A non-empty consumerId is required to subscribe or unsubscribe.");
    75	  }
    76	}
    77	
    78	function pollerKey(descriptor) {
    79	  return JSON.stringify([descriptor.marketKind, descriptor.symbol, descriptor.eventType, descriptor.interval ?? null]);
    80	}
    81	
    82	function createBinanceRestConnection(options = {}) {
    83	  const {
    84	    telemetry,
    85	    fetchImpl = globalThis.fetch,
    86	    endpoints = DEFAULT_BINANCE_ENDPOINTS,
    87	    pollMs = DEFAULT_POLL_MS,
    88	    weightBudgetPerMinute = DEFAULT_WEIGHT_BUDGET_PER_MINUTE,
    89	    now = () => Date.now(),
    90	    random = Math.random,
    91	    probeSymbol = listAllowedSymbols()[0],
    92	    requestTimeoutMs = DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS,
    93	    clockRefreshMs = DEFAULT_CLOCK_REFRESH_MS,
    94	    maxCatchUpPages = DEFAULT_MAX_CATCH_UP_PAGES,
    95	    finalizeGraceMs = DEFAULT_FINALIZE_GRACE_MS,
    96	    defaultBanMs = DEFAULT_BAN_MS,
    97	  } = options;
    98	
    99	  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(pollMs) || pollMs < MIN_POLL_MS) {
   100	    throw new Error(`pollMs must be a safe integer of at least ${MIN_POLL_MS}.`);
   101	  }
   102	  for (const [name, value] of [
   103	    ["requestTimeoutMs", requestTimeoutMs],
   104	    ["clockRefreshMs", clockRefreshMs],
   105	    ["maxCatchUpPages", maxCatchUpPages],
   106	    ["defaultBanMs", defaultBanMs],
   107	  ]) {
   108	    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(value) || value <= 0) throw new Error(`${name} must be a positive safe integer.`);
   109	  }
   110	  if (typeof now !== "function" || typeof random !== "function") {
   111	    throw new Error("now and random must be functions.");
   112	  }
   113	  const resolvedEndpoints = resolveBinanceEndpoints(endpoints);
   114	  const spotBaseUrl = restBaseUrlFor(MARKET_KINDS.SPOT, resolvedEndpoints);
   115	  const marketKindContexts = Object.freeze({
   116	    SPOT: createBinanceMarketKindContext(MARKET_KINDS.SPOT, resolvedEndpoints),
   117	    PERPETUAL: createBinanceMarketKindContext(MARKET_KINDS.PERPETUAL, resolvedEndpoints),
   118	  });
   119	  const feedHealth = createBinanceFeedHealthMonitor({ now });
   120	  const budget = createBinanceRestWeightBudget({ budgetPerMinute: weightBudgetPerMinute, now });
   121	  // The backfill classifies candles against this transport's exchange clock
   122	  // with its grace, and every page goes through the shared dispatch gate.
   123	  const klineBackfill = createBinanceKlineBackfill({
   124	    fetchKlinesImpl: gatedFetchKlines,
   125	    telemetry,
   126	    endpoints: resolvedEndpoints,
   127	    now: exchangeNow,
   128	    finalizeGraceMs,
   129	  });
   130	
   131	  const messageListeners = new Set();
   132	  const errorListeners = new Set();
   133	  const pollers = new Map();
   134	  const probe = { inflight: null, timer: null, attempt: 0 };
   135	  const clock = { inflight: null, timer: null };
   136	  const gatedControllers = new Set();
   137	  let accepting = false;
   138	  let sessionGeneration = 0;
   139	  let routeState = "DISCONNECTED";
   140	  let exchangeOffsetMs = 0;
   141	  let clockSyncedAtMs = null;
   142	  let banUntilMs = 0;
   143	  let rateLimitedUntilMs = 0;
   144	  let consecutiveTransportFailures = 0;
   145	  let plan = planCadence({ klinePollers: 0, pollMs, budgetPerMinute: weightBudgetPerMinute });
   146	  const counters = { polls: 0, pollFailures: 0, budgetDeferrals: 0, emitted: 0, probes: 0, clockSyncs: 0, clockRefreshFailures: 0 };
   147	
   148	  /** The exchange's clock as this process last measured it; rows are classified against it, never against the machine. */
   149	  function exchangeNow() {
   150	    return now() + exchangeOffsetMs;
   151	  }
   152	
   153	  function telemetryEvent(name, fields = {}) {
   154	    emitTelemetry(telemetry, `market_data.binance_rest.${name}`, fields);
   155	  }
   156	
   157	  function safelyInvokeListener(listener, payload) {
   158	    try {
   159	      listener(payload);
   160	    } catch (error) {
   161	      console.error("binance rest listener failed", error?.message ?? error);
   162	    }
   163	  }
   164	
   165	  function notifyMessageListeners(envelope) {
   166	    counters.emitted += 1;
   167	    for (const listener of [...messageListeners]) safelyInvokeListener(listener, envelope);
   168	  }
   169	
   170	  function notifyErrorListeners(payload) {
   171	    for (const listener of [...errorListeners]) safelyInvokeListener(listener, Object.freeze(payload));
   172	  }
   173	
   174	  /** Resolves true after `ms`, or false at once when `signal` aborts first. */
   175	  function sleep(ms, signal) {
   176	    return new Promise((resolve) => {
   177	      if (signal.aborted) {
   178	        resolve(false);
   179	        return;
   180	      }
   181	      const timer = setTimeout(() => {
   182	        signal.removeEventListener("abort", onAbort);
   183	        resolve(true);
   184	      }, Math.max(1, ms));
   185	      timer.unref?.();
   186	      function onAbort() {
   187	        clearTimeout(timer);
   188	        resolve(false);
   189	      }
   190	      signal.addEventListener("abort", onAbort, { once: true });
   191	    });
   192	  }
   193	
   194	  // The dispatch gate every request of this transport passes: it waits out a
   195	  // ban or rate-limit pause, then reserves the request's weight, waiting for
   196	  // the next minute when the budget is spent. False means the wait was aborted.
   197	  async function awaitDispatch(weight, signal) {
   198	    for (;;) {
   199	      if (signal.aborted) return false;
   200	      const wait = waitBeforeDispatchMs();
   201	      if (wait > 0) {
   202	        if (!(await sleep(wait, signal))) return false;
   203	        continue;
   204	      }
   205	      if (budget.reserve(weight)) return true;
   206	      counters.budgetDeferrals += 1;
   207	      if (!(await sleep(budget.msUntilReset(), signal))) return false;
   208	    }
   209	  }
   210	
   211	  // A request issued on behalf of another module (the candle backfill, the
   212	  // tick-size lookup): gated, weighed, its headers read back, its failure fed
   213	  // to the shared rate-limit, ban and route handling, and abortable by the
   214	  // caller's signal or by disconnect().
   215	  async function gatedRequest(weight, externalSignal, perform) {
   216	    if (externalSignal !== undefined && !(externalSignal instanceof AbortSignal)) {
   217	      throw new Error("signal must be an AbortSignal when provided");
   218	    }
   219	    const controller = new AbortController();
   220	    const forwardAbort = () => controller.abort();
   221	    if (externalSignal?.aborted) controller.abort();
   222	    else externalSignal?.addEventListener("abort", forwardAbort, { once: true });
   223	    gatedControllers.add(controller);
   224	    try {
   225	      if (!(await awaitDispatch(weight, controller.signal))) return Object.freeze({ ok: false, reason: "ABORTED" });
   226	      let result;
   227	      try {
   228	        result = await perform(controller.signal);
   229	      } catch (error) {
   230	        result = Object.freeze({ ok: false, reason: error?.name === "AbortError" ? "ABORTED" : "NETWORK_ERROR" });
   231	      }
   232	      observeWeight(result);
   233	      if (!result.ok && result.reason !== "ABORTED") noteRequestFailure(result);
   234	      return result;
   235	    } finally {
   236	      gatedControllers.delete(controller);
   237	      externalSignal?.removeEventListener("abort", forwardAbort);
   238	    }
   239	  }
   240	
   241	  function gatedFetchKlines(marketKind, symbol, options = {}) {
   242	    return gatedRequest(REQUEST_WEIGHTS.KLINES, options.signal, (signal) =>
   243	      fetchKlines(marketKind, symbol, { ...options, fetchImpl, timeoutMs: requestTimeoutMs, signal }));
   244	  }
   245	
   246	  /** exchangeInfo for another module (the tick-size resolver), charged 20 through the same gate. */
   247	  function fetchExchangeInfoGated(marketKind, symbol, options = {}) {
   248	    return gatedRequest(REQUEST_WEIGHTS.EXCHANGE_INFO, options.signal, (signal) =>
   249	      fetchExchangeInfo(marketKind, symbol, {
   250	        ...options,
   251	        fetchImpl,
   252	        baseUrl: restBaseUrlFor(marketKind, resolvedEndpoints),
   253	        timeoutMs: requestTimeoutMs,
   254	        signal,
   255	      }));
   256	  }
   257	
   258	  function setRouteState(next, fields = {}) {
   259	    if (routeState === next) return;
   260	    routeState = next;
   261	    if (next === "CONNECTED") feedHealth.recordRouteConnected(MARKET_KINDS.SPOT, ROUTE_NAME);
   262	    else feedHealth.recordRouteDisconnected(MARKET_KINDS.SPOT, ROUTE_NAME);
   263	    telemetryEvent(next === "CONNECTED" ? "route_connected" : "route_disconnected", { state: next, ...fields });
   264	  }
   265	
   266	  function backoffDelayMs(attempt) {
   267	    const raw = Math.min(BACKOFF_BASE_DELAY_MS * 2 ** Math.max(0, attempt - 1), BACKOFF_MAX_DELAY_MS);
   268	    const jitter = raw * BACKOFF_JITTER_RATIO * (random() * 2 - 1);
   269	    return Math.max(0, Math.round(raw + jitter));
   270	  }
   271	
   272	  function clearTimer(holder) {
   273	    if (holder.timer) clearTimeout(holder.timer);
   274	    holder.timer = null;
   275	  }
   276	
   277	  function schedule(holder, delayMs, run) {
   278	    clearTimer(holder);
   279	    const generationAtSchedule = sessionGeneration;
   280	    holder.timer = setTimeout(() => {
   281	      holder.timer = null;
   282	      if (!accepting || generationAtSchedule !== sessionGeneration) return;
   283	      run().catch((error) => {
   284	        notifyErrorListeners({ marketKind: MARKET_KINDS.SPOT, route: ROUTE_NAME, message: error?.message ?? String(error), code: "REST_INTERNAL" });
   285	      });
   286	    }, Math.max(0, delayMs));
   287	    holder.timer.unref?.();
   288	  }
   289	
   290	  function waitBeforeDispatchMs() {
   291	    const at = now();
   292	    return Math.max(0, banUntilMs - at, rateLimitedUntilMs - at);
   293	  }
   294	
   295	  function requestOptions(signal) {
   296	    return { fetchImpl, baseUrl: spotBaseUrl, timeoutMs: requestTimeoutMs, signal };
   297	  }
   298	
   299	  function observeWeight(result) {
   300	    const headers = result.ok ? result.value.headers : result.headers;
   301	    if (headers) budget.observe(headers);
   302	  }
   303	
   304	  function applyServerTime(serverTime, sentAt, receivedAt) {
   305	    exchangeOffsetMs = serverTime - Math.round((sentAt + receivedAt) / 2);
   306	    clockSyncedAtMs = receivedAt;
   307	    counters.clockSyncs += 1;
   308	  }
   309	
   310	  // The route is up once exchangeInfo (weight 20) answers: it proves the
   311	  // origin, the proxy and the symbol, and its serverTime seeds the exchange
   312	  // clock every candle is classified against.
   313	  async function runProbe() {
   314	    if (!accepting || probe.inflight) return;
   315	    const wait = waitBeforeDispatchMs();
   316	    if (wait > 0) {
   317	      schedule(probe, wait, runProbe);
   318	      return;
   319	    }
   320	    if (!budget.reserve(REQUEST_WEIGHTS.EXCHANGE_INFO)) {
   321	      counters.budgetDeferrals += 1;
   322	      schedule(probe, budget.msUntilReset(), runProbe);
   323	      return;
   324	    }
   325	    const controller = new AbortController();
   326	    probe.inflight = controller;
   327	    const generation = sessionGeneration;
   328	    const sentAt = now();
   329	    counters.probes += 1;
   330	    let result;
   331	    try {
   332	      result = await fetchExchangeInfo(MARKET_KINDS.SPOT, probeSymbol, requestOptions(controller.signal));
   333	    } catch {
   334	      result = Object.freeze({ ok: false, reason: "NETWORK_ERROR" });
   335	    }
   336	    probe.inflight = null;
   337	    if (!accepting || generation !== sessionGeneration) return;
   338	    const receivedAt = now();
   339	    observeWeight(result);
   340	    const serverTime = result.ok ? result.value.body?.serverTime : undefined;
   341	    if (!result.ok || !Number.isSafeInteger(serverTime)) {
   342	      probe.attempt += 1;
   343	      telemetryEvent("probe_failed", { reason: result.ok ? "MALFORMED_RESPONSE_BODY" : result.reason, attempt: probe.attempt });
   344	      if (!result.ok) noteRequestFailure(result);
   345	      schedule(probe, Math.max(waitBeforeDispatchMs(), backoffDelayMs(probe.attempt)), runProbe);
   346	      return;
   347	    }
   348	    probe.attempt = 0;
   349	    applyServerTime(serverTime, sentAt, receivedAt);
   350	    consecutiveTransportFailures = 0;
   351	    setRouteState("CONNECTED");
   352	    scheduleClockRefresh();
   353	    for (const poller of pollers.values()) schedulePoll(poller, 0);
   354	  }
   355	
   356	  // The exchange clock is re-read from /api/v3/time (weight 1, reserved like
   357	  // every request) once a minute; a failed refresh keeps the last offset.
   358	  function scheduleClockRefresh() {
   359	    schedule(clock, clockRefreshMs, refreshClock);
   360	  }
   361	
   362	  async function refreshClock() {
   363	    if (!accepting || routeState !== "CONNECTED" || clock.inflight) return;
   364	    if (waitBeforeDispatchMs() > 0 || !budget.reserve(REQUEST_WEIGHTS.SERVER_TIME)) {
   365	      counters.budgetDeferrals += 1;
   366	      scheduleClockRefresh();
   367	      return;
   368	    }
   369	    const controller = new AbortController();
   370	    clock.inflight = controller;
   371	    const generation = sessionGeneration;
   372	    const sentAt = now();
   373	    let result;
   374	    try {
   375	      result = await fetchServerTime(MARKET_KINDS.SPOT, requestOptions(controller.signal));
   376	    } catch {
   377	      result = Object.freeze({ ok: false, reason: "NETWORK_ERROR" });
   378	    }
   379	    clock.inflight = null;
   380	    if (!accepting || generation !== sessionGeneration) return;
   381	    observeWeight(result);
   382	    if (result.ok && Number.isSafeInteger(result.value.body?.serverTime)) {
   383	      applyServerTime(result.value.body.serverTime, sentAt, now());
   384	    } else {
   385	      // The last valid offset stays; the failure itself gets the shared
   386	      // handling, so a 429 pauses polling and a 418 opens the ban window.
   387	      counters.clockRefreshFailures += 1;
   388	      telemetryEvent("clock_refresh_failed", { reason: result.ok ? "MALFORMED_RESPONSE_BODY" : result.reason, status: result.status ?? null });
   389	      if (!result.ok && result.reason !== "ABORTED") noteRequestFailure(result);
   390	    }
   391	    scheduleClockRefresh();
   392	  }
   393	
   394	  // Bookkeeping shared by every failed request: 429 pauses everything until
   395	  // Retry-After (or the backoff); 418 is an IP ban that stops polling for the
   396	  // whole window; three transport failures in a row mean the route is down.
   397	  function noteRequestFailure(result) {
   398	    counters.pollFailures += 1;
   399	    const at = now();
   400	    if (result.reason === "IP_BANNED") {
   401	      banUntilMs = Math.max(banUntilMs, at + (result.retryAfterMs ?? defaultBanMs));
   402	      telemetryEvent("ip_banned", { untilMs: banUntilMs, retryAfterMs: result.retryAfterMs ?? null });
   403	      notifyErrorListeners({ marketKind: MARKET_KINDS.SPOT, route: ROUTE_NAME, message: "Binance banned this IP; polling stops for the Retry-After window.", code: 418 });
   404	      setRouteState("DISCONNECTED", { reason: "IP_BANNED" });
   405	      schedule(probe, banUntilMs - at, runProbe);
   406	      return;
   407	    }
   408	    if (result.reason === "RATE_LIMITED") {
   409	      rateLimitedUntilMs = Math.max(rateLimitedUntilMs, at + (result.retryAfterMs ?? backoffDelayMs(1)));
   410	      telemetryEvent("rate_limited", { untilMs: rateLimitedUntilMs, retryAfterMs: result.retryAfterMs ?? null });
   411	      return;
   412	    }
   413	    if (TRANSPORT_FAILURE_REASONS.has(result.reason)) {
   414	      consecutiveTransportFailures += 1;
   415	      if (routeState === "CONNECTED" && consecutiveTransportFailures >= CONSECUTIVE_FAILURES_BEFORE_DISCONNECT) {
   416	        notifyErrorListeners({ marketKind: MARKET_KINDS.SPOT, route: ROUTE_NAME, message: "Binance REST polling failed repeatedly; the route is down until a probe succeeds." });
   417	        setRouteState("DISCONNECTED", { reason: result.reason });
   418	        schedule(probe, backoffDelayMs(1), runProbe);
   419	      }
   420	    }
   421	  }
   422	
   423	  function replan() {
   424	    plan = planCadence({ klinePollers: pollers.size, pollMs, budgetPerMinute: weightBudgetPerMinute });
   425	  }
   426	
   427	  function createPoller(descriptor) {
   428	    return {
   429	      descriptor,
   430	      key: pollerKey(descriptor),
   431	      timer: null,
   432	      inflight: null,
   433	      epoch: 0,
   434	      seq: 0,
   435	      attempt: 0,
   436	      catchUpPages: 0,
   437	      catchingUp: false,
   438	      lastFailureReason: null,
   439	      lastLatencyMs: null,
   440	      lastSuccessAtMs: null,
   441	      lastSentAtMs: null,
   442	      budgetDeferred: false,
   443	      bars: createBinanceRestBarCursor({ intervalMs: candleIntervalMsFor(descriptor.interval), finalizeGraceMs }),
   444	    };
   445	  }
   446	
   447	  function resetPollerState(poller) {
   448	    poller.bars.reset();
   449	    poller.catchUpPages = 0;
   450	    poller.catchingUp = false;
   451	    poller.attempt = 0;
   452	  }
   453	
   454	  function schedulePoll(poller, delayMs) {
   455	    schedule(poller, delayMs, () => runPoll(poller));
   456	  }
   457	
   458	  function scheduleNextPoll(poller, { immediate = false } = {}) {
   459	    if (immediate) {
   460	      schedulePoll(poller, 0);
   461	      return;
   462	    }
   463	    const elapsed = poller.lastSentAtMs === null ? 0 : now() - poller.lastSentAtMs;
   464	    schedulePoll(poller, Math.max(0, plan.klineCadenceMs - elapsed));
   465	  }
   466	
   467	  function beginRecovery(poller) {
   468	    const { marketKind, symbol, eventType, interval } = poller.descriptor;
   469	    feedHealth.beginRecovery(marketKind, symbol, eventType, interval);
   470	  }
   471	
   472	  function endRecovery(poller) {
   473	    const { marketKind, symbol, eventType, interval } = poller.descriptor;
   474	    feedHealth.endRecovery(marketKind, symbol, eventType, interval);
   475	  }
   476	
   477	  async function runPoll(poller) {
   478	    if (!accepting || routeState !== "CONNECTED" || poller.inflight || pollers.get(poller.key) !== poller) return;
   479	    const wait = waitBeforeDispatchMs();
   480	    if (wait > 0) {
   481	      schedulePoll(poller, wait);
   482	      return;
   483	    }
   484	    // Weight is reserved before dispatch; a refusal waits for the next minute.
   485	    if (!budget.reserve(REQUEST_WEIGHTS.KLINES)) {
   486	      counters.budgetDeferrals += 1;
   487	      poller.budgetDeferred = true;
   488	      telemetryEvent("poll_deferred_by_budget", { symbol: safeTelemetrySymbol(poller.descriptor.symbol) });
   489	      schedulePoll(poller, budget.msUntilReset());
   490	      return;
   491	    }
   492	    poller.budgetDeferred = false;
   493	    const controller = new AbortController();
   494	    const epochAtRequest = poller.epoch;
   495	    const generationAtRequest = sessionGeneration;
   496	    const seq = ++poller.seq;
   497	    poller.inflight = controller;
   498	    poller.lastSentAtMs = now();
   499	    counters.polls += 1;
   500	    const request = poller.bars.nextRequest(exchangeNow());
   501	    let result;
   502	    try {
   503	      result = await fetchKlines(MARKET_KINDS.SPOT, poller.descriptor.symbol, {
   504	        ...requestOptions(controller.signal),
   505	        interval: poller.descriptor.interval,
   506	        startTime: request.startTime,
   507	        limit: request.limit,
   508	      });
   509	    } catch (error) {
   510	      result = Object.freeze({ ok: false, reason: error?.name === "AbortError" ? "ABORTED" : "NETWORK_ERROR" });
   511	    }
   512	    if (poller.inflight === controller) poller.inflight = null;
   513	    // A response for a subscription that ended, or from a session that was
   514	    // disconnected, is thrown away whatever it says.
   515	    if (!accepting || generationAtRequest !== sessionGeneration || epochAtRequest !== poller.epoch || pollers.get(poller.key) !== poller) return;
   516	    const receivedAt = now();
   517	    poller.lastLatencyMs = receivedAt - poller.lastSentAtMs;
   518	    observeWeight(result);
   519	    if (!result.ok) {
   520	      onPollFailure(poller, result);
   521	      return;
   522	    }
   523	    const outcome = applyKlinePage(poller, result.value.body, receivedAt, seq);
   524	    if (outcome.malformed) {
   525	      onPollFailure(poller, Object.freeze({ ok: false, reason: "MALFORMED_RESPONSE_BODY" }));
   526	      return;
   527	    }
   528	    consecutiveTransportFailures = 0;
   529	    poller.attempt = 0;
   530	    poller.lastFailureReason = null;
   531	    poller.lastSuccessAtMs = receivedAt;
   532	    if (outcome.complete) endRecovery(poller);
   533	    scheduleNextPoll(poller, { immediate: outcome.immediate });
   534	  }
   535	
   536	  function onPollFailure(poller, result) {
   537	    poller.attempt += 1;
   538	    poller.lastFailureReason = result.reason;
   539	    poller.catchUpPages = 0;
   540	    beginRecovery(poller);
   541	    telemetryEvent("poll_failed", {
   542	      symbol: safeTelemetrySymbol(poller.descriptor.symbol),
   543	      reason: result.reason,
   544	      status: result.status ?? null,
   545	      attempt: poller.attempt,
   546	    });
   547	    noteRequestFailure(result);
   548	    if (routeState !== "CONNECTED") return;
   549	    schedulePoll(poller, Math.max(waitBeforeDispatchMs(), backoffDelayMs(poller.attempt)));
   550	  }
   551	
   552	  function emitEnvelope(poller, raw, receivedAtMs) {
   553	    const { marketKind, symbol, eventType, interval } = poller.descriptor;
   554	    const envelope = normalizeBinanceEvent({ marketKind, symbol, eventType, interval, raw }, { receivedAtMs });
   555	    const health = feedHealth.computeContext({ marketKind, symbol, eventType, interval, routeConnected: routeState === "CONNECTED", nowMs: now() });
   556	    notifyMessageListeners(Object.freeze({ ...envelope, stale: health.stale, recoveryState: health.recoveryState }));
   557	  }
   558	
   559	  function applyKlinePage(poller, body, receivedAt, seq) {
   560	    const { marketKind, symbol, eventType, interval } = poller.descriptor;
   561	    const rows = parseKlineRows(body, candleIntervalMsFor(interval));
   562	    if (rows === null) return { malformed: true };
   563	    // A successful poll is an observation of the pair; only a failed poll
   564	    // leaves the feed to go stale, as a dead stream would.
   565	    feedHealth.recordEvent(marketKind, symbol, eventType, interval, receivedAt);
   566	    const outcome = poller.bars.apply({ rows, exchangeNowMs: exchangeNow(), seq });
   567	    if (outcome.stale) {
   568	      telemetryEvent("kline_poll_superseded", { symbol: safeTelemetrySymbol(symbol) });
   569	      return { malformed: false, complete: !poller.catchingUp, immediate: false };
   570	    }
   571	    if (outcome.hole) telemetryEvent("kline_hole", { symbol: safeTelemetrySymbol(symbol), confirmedOpenTime: poller.bars.confirmedOpenTime });
   572	    for (const { row, closed } of outcome.emit) {
   573	      emitEnvelope(poller, {
   574	        e: "kline",
   575	        E: receivedAt,
   576	        s: symbol,
   577	        k: {
   578	          t: row.openTime,
   579	          T: row.closeTime,
   580	          s: symbol,
   581	          i: interval,
   582	          o: row.open,
   583	          c: row.close,
   584	          h: row.high,
   585	          l: row.low,
   586	          v: row.volume,
   587	          n: row.numberOfTrades,
   588	          x: closed,
   589	          q: row.quoteVolume,
   590	          V: row.takerBuyBaseVolume,
   591	          Q: row.takerBuyQuoteVolume,
   592	        },
   593	      }, receivedAt);
   594	    }
   595	    if (outcome.catchingUp) {
   596	      // Behind by more than one bar: the next request continues from the
   597	      // confirmed close, in pages of up to 1000, at most maxCatchUpPages in a
   598	      // row before the poller yields for a cadence and continues.
   599	      if (!poller.catchingUp) telemetryEvent("kline_catch_up_started", { symbol: safeTelemetrySymbol(symbol), confirmedOpenTime: poller.bars.confirmedOpenTime });
   600	      poller.catchingUp = true;
   601	      beginRecovery(poller);
   602	      poller.catchUpPages += 1;
   603	      if (poller.catchUpPages >= maxCatchUpPages) {
   604	        poller.catchUpPages = 0;
   605	        return { malformed: false, complete: false, immediate: false };
   606	      }
   607	      return { malformed: false, complete: false, immediate: true };
   608	    }
   609	    if (poller.catchingUp) telemetryEvent("kline_catch_up_completed", { symbol: safeTelemetrySymbol(symbol), confirmedOpenTime: poller.bars.confirmedOpenTime });
   610	    poller.catchingUp = false;
   611	    poller.catchUpPages = 0;
   612	    return { malformed: false, complete: true, immediate: false };
   613	  }
   614	
   615	  function connect() {
   616	    accepting = true;
   617	    for (const poller of pollers.values()) resetPollerState(poller);
   618	    if (routeState === "DISCONNECTED") routeState = "CONNECTING";
   619	    schedule(probe, 0, runProbe);
   620	  }
   621	
   622	  function abortAll() {
   623	    for (const holder of [probe, clock, ...pollers.values()]) {
   624	      clearTimer(holder);
   625	      holder.inflight?.abort();
   626	      holder.inflight = null;
   627	    }
   628	    for (const controller of gatedControllers) controller.abort();
   629	  }
   630	
   631	  function disconnect() {
   632	    accepting = false;
   633	    sessionGeneration += 1;
   634	    abortAll();
   635	    probe.attempt = 0;
   636	    banUntilMs = 0;
   637	    rateLimitedUntilMs = 0;
   638	    consecutiveTransportFailures = 0;
   639	    for (const poller of pollers.values()) resetPollerState(poller);
   640	    setRouteState("DISCONNECTED", { reason: "DISCONNECT" });
   641	  }
   642	
   643	  function getStatus() {
   644	    return Object.freeze({
   645	      SPOT: Object.freeze({ public: routeState }),
   646	      PERPETUAL: PERPETUAL_STATUS,
   647	    });
   648	  }
   649	
   650	  function registryFor(marketKind) {
   651	    return marketKindContexts[marketKind].subscriptionRegistry;
   652	  }
   653	
   654	  function subscribeMany(requests, consumerId) {
   655	    assertConsumerId(consumerId);
   656	    if (!Array.isArray(requests) || requests.length === 0) {
   657	      throw new Error("subscribeMany() requires a non-empty array of subscription requests.");
   658	    }
   659	    const descriptors = requests.map((request) => describeBinanceSubscription(request));
   660	    const added = [];
   661	    const rollback = () => {
   662	      for (const descriptor of added) {
   663	        registryFor(descriptor.marketKind).remove(descriptor.symbol, descriptor.eventType, consumerId, descriptor.interval);
   664	      }
   665	    };
   666	    for (const descriptor of descriptors) {
   667	      const isNewOnWire = registryFor(descriptor.marketKind).add(descriptor.symbol, descriptor.eventType, consumerId, undefined, descriptor.interval);
   668	      if (isNewOnWire) added.push(descriptor);
   669	    }
   670	    for (const descriptor of descriptors) {
   671	      // Perpetuals have no REST mirror and are not served by this transport;
   672	      // their route never connects, exactly as the stream transport reports a
   673	      // route that is not up.
   674	      if (descriptor.marketKind === MARKET_KINDS.PERPETUAL || routeState !== "CONNECTED") {
   675	        rollback();
   676	        throw new Error(`Cannot subscribe before the Binance ${descriptor.marketKind} ${ROUTE_NAME} route is connected.`);
   677	      }
   678	    }
   679	    for (const descriptor of added) {
   680	      // Trades, book ticker and depth are accepted so the server's bookkeeping
   681	      // is unchanged, but nothing is polled for them: they are unavailable.
   682	      if (!SERVED_EVENT_TYPES.has(descriptor.eventType)) continue;
   683	      const poller = createPoller(descriptor);
   684	      pollers.set(poller.key, poller);
   685	      beginRecovery(poller);
   686	      schedulePoll(poller, 0);
   687	    }
   688	    replan();
   689	    return added.length;
   690	  }
   691	
   692	  function subscribe(request, consumerId) {
   693	    return subscribeMany([request], consumerId) === 1;
   694	  }
   695	
   696	  function teardownDescriptor(descriptor) {
   697	    const key = pollerKey(descriptor);
   698	    const poller = pollers.get(key);
   699	    if (!poller) return;
   700	    pollers.delete(key);
   701	    poller.epoch += 1;
   702	    clearTimer(poller);
   703	    poller.inflight?.abort();
   704	    poller.inflight = null;
   705	    feedHealth.endRecovery(descriptor.marketKind, descriptor.symbol, descriptor.eventType, descriptor.interval);
   706	  }
   707	
   708	  function unsubscribeMany(requests, consumerId) {
   709	    assertConsumerId(consumerId);
   710	    if (!Array.isArray(requests) || requests.length === 0) {
   711	      throw new Error("unsubscribeMany() requires a non-empty array of subscription requests.");
   712	    }
   713	    const descriptors = requests.map((request) => describeBinanceSubscription(request));
   714	    let released = 0;
   715	    for (const descriptor of descriptors) {
   716	      const lastHolderReleased = registryFor(descriptor.marketKind).remove(descriptor.symbol, descriptor.eventType, consumerId, descriptor.interval);
   717	      if (!lastHolderReleased) continue;
   718	      released += 1;
   719	      teardownDescriptor(descriptor);
   720	    }
   721	    replan();
   722	    return released;
   723	  }
   724	
   725	  function unsubscribe(request, consumerId) {
   726	    return unsubscribeMany([request], consumerId) === 1;
   727	  }
   728	
   729	  function removeConsumer(consumerId) {
   730	    assertConsumerId(consumerId);
   731	    const released = [];
   732	    for (const marketKind of Object.values(MARKET_KINDS)) {
   733	      for (const tuple of registryFor(marketKind).removeConsumer(consumerId)) {
   734	        const descriptor = Object.freeze({
   735	          symbol: tuple.symbol,
   736	          marketKind,
   737	          eventType: tuple.eventType,
   738	          ...(tuple.interval !== undefined ? { interval: tuple.interval } : {}),
   739	        });
   740	        teardownDescriptor(descriptor);
   741	        released.push(descriptor);
   742	      }
   743	    }
   744	    replan();
   745	    return released;
   746	  }
   747	
   748	  function isSubscribed(marketKind, symbol, eventType, interval) {
   749	    const context = marketKindContexts[marketKind];
   750	    if (!context) return false;
   751	    return context.subscriptionRegistry.list().some(
   752	      (entry) => entry.symbol === symbol && entry.eventType === eventType && (entry.interval ?? null) === (interval ?? null),
   753	    );
   754	  }
   755	
   756	  function recoverStaleSubscription(request, { automatic = false } = {}) {
   757	    if (typeof automatic !== "boolean") {
   758	      throw new Error("recoverStaleSubscription(): automatic must be a boolean when provided.");
   759	    }
   760	    const descriptor = describeBinanceSubscription(request);
   761	    const poller = pollers.get(pollerKey(descriptor));
   762	    if (!poller || routeState !== "CONNECTED" || poller.inflight || waitBeforeDispatchMs() > 0) return false;
   763	    telemetryEvent("stale_subscription_recovery_started", { eventType: descriptor.eventType, symbol: safeTelemetrySymbol(descriptor.symbol) });
   764	    beginRecovery(poller);
   765	    poller.attempt = 0;
   766	    schedulePoll(poller, 0);
   767	    return true;
   768	  }
   769	
   770	  function healthVariants(poller) {
   771	    const variants = [];
   772	    const at = now();
   773	    if (at < banUntilMs || at < rateLimitedUntilMs) variants.push(HEALTH_REASON_VARIANTS.RATE_LIMITED);
   774	    const pollFailed = poller !== null && poller.lastFailureReason !== null &&
   775	      poller.lastFailureReason !== "RATE_LIMITED" && poller.lastFailureReason !== "IP_BANNED";
   776	    if (routeState !== "CONNECTED" || pollFailed) variants.push(HEALTH_REASON_VARIANTS.POLL_FAILED);
   777	    if (poller !== null && poller.catchingUp) variants.push(HEALTH_REASON_VARIANTS.CATCHING_UP);
   778	    if (plan.stretched || poller?.budgetDeferred) variants.push(HEALTH_REASON_VARIANTS.SLOW_POLLING);
   779	    return variants;
   780	  }
   781	
   782	  function healthReason(poller) {
   783	    return [HEALTH_REASON_BASE, ...healthVariants(poller)].join(" · ");
   784	  }
   785	
   786	  function getFeedHealth(marketKind, symbol, eventType, interval) {
   787	    if (!isSubscribed(marketKind, symbol, eventType, interval)) return undefined;
   788	    if (marketKind !== MARKET_KINDS.SPOT || !SERVED_EVENT_TYPES.has(eventType)) {
   789	      // Not served at all: unavailable, and flagged so the server's worst-of
   790	      // health follows the candle feed while the reason names what is missing.
   791	      return Object.freeze({ stale: true, recoveryState: "UNAVAILABLE", transport: TRANSPORTS.REST, served: false, reason: healthReason(null) });
   792	    }
   793	    const poller = pollers.get(pollerKey({ marketKind, symbol, eventType, interval })) ?? null;
   794	    const context = feedHealth.computeContext({ marketKind, symbol, eventType, interval, routeConnected: routeState === "CONNECTED" });
   795	    return Object.freeze({
   796	      ...context,
   797	      transport: TRANSPORTS.REST,
   798	      served: true,
   799	      reason: healthReason(poller),
   800	      pollMs: plan.klineCadenceMs,
   801	      lastPollLatencyMs: poller?.lastLatencyMs ?? null,
   802	    });
   803	  }
   804	
   805	  function getOrderBook(marketKind, symbol) {
   806	    return isSubscribed(marketKind, symbol, EVENT_TYPES.DEPTH) ? BOOK_UNAVAILABLE : undefined;
   807	  }
   808	
   809	  function getDepthSnapshot() {
   810	    return undefined;
   811	  }
   812	
   813	  function onMessage(listener) {
   814	    if (typeof listener !== "function") throw new Error("Binance message listener must be a function.");
   815	    if (!messageListeners.has(listener) && messageListeners.size >= MAX_LISTENERS) {
   816	      throw new Error(`Cannot register more than ${MAX_LISTENERS} Binance message listeners.`);
   817	    }
   818	    messageListeners.add(listener);
   819	    return () => messageListeners.delete(listener);
   820	  }
   821	
   822	  function onError(listener) {
   823	    if (typeof listener !== "function") throw new Error("Binance error listener must be a function.");
   824	    if (!errorListeners.has(listener) && errorListeners.size >= MAX_LISTENERS) {
   825	      throw new Error(`Cannot register more than ${MAX_LISTENERS} Binance error listeners.`);
   826	    }
   827	    errorListeners.add(listener);
   828	    return () => errorListeners.delete(listener);
   829	  }
   830	
   831	  function getOperationalStats() {
   832	    let inflight = 0;
   833	    const pollerStats = [];
   834	    for (const poller of pollers.values()) {
   835	      if (poller.inflight) inflight += 1;
   836	      pollerStats.push(Object.freeze({
   837	        symbol: poller.descriptor.symbol,
   838	        interval: poller.descriptor.interval,
   839	        cadenceMs: plan.klineCadenceMs,
   840	        lastLatencyMs: poller.lastLatencyMs,
   841	        lastSuccessAtMs: poller.lastSuccessAtMs,
   842	        lastFailureReason: poller.lastFailureReason,
   843	        catchingUp: poller.catchingUp,
   844	        confirmedOpenTime: poller.bars.confirmedOpenTime,
   845	      }));
   846	    }
   847	    return Object.freeze({
   848	      transport: TRANSPORTS.REST,
   849	      routeState,
   850	      pollMs,
   851	      klineCadenceMs: plan.klineCadenceMs,
   852	      cadenceStretched: plan.stretched,
   853	      projectedWeightPerMinute: plan.projectedPerMinute,
   854	      weight: budget.stats(),
   855	      ...counters,
   856	      inflight: inflight + (probe.inflight ? 1 : 0) + (clock.inflight ? 1 : 0) + gatedControllers.size,
   857	      gatedRequests: gatedControllers.size,
   858	      exchangeOffsetMs,
   859	      clockSyncedAtMs,
   860	      rateLimitedUntilMs,
   861	      banUntilMs,
   862	      pollers: Object.freeze(pollerStats),
   863	    });
   864	  }
   865	
   866	  function quiesce({ timeoutMs = 30_000 } = {}) {
   867	    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(timeoutMs) || timeoutMs <= 0) {
   868	      return Promise.reject(new Error("quiesce timeoutMs must be a positive safe integer."));
   869	    }
   870	    accepting = false;
   871	    for (const holder of [probe, clock, ...pollers.values()]) clearTimer(holder);
   872	    // A backfill or lookup still queued at the gate would wait for a minute
   873	    // boundary or a ban to end; shutdown does not.
   874	    for (const controller of gatedControllers) controller.abort();
   875	    const deadline = now() + timeoutMs;
   876	    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
   877	      const inspect = () => {
   878	        const { inflight } = getOperationalStats();
   879	        if (inflight === 0) {
   880	          resolve();
   881	          return;
   882	        }
   883	        if (now() >= deadline) {
   884	          reject(new Error(`Binance REST connection still has ${inflight} request(s) in flight at shutdown.`));
   885	          return;
   886	        }
   887	        setTimeout(inspect, 10);
   888	      };
   889	      inspect();
   890	    });
   891	  }
   892	
   893	  function fetchRecentKlines(marketKind, symbol, klineOptions) {
   894	    return klineBackfill.fetchRecentBars(marketKind, symbol, klineOptions);
   895	  }
   896	
   897	  return Object.freeze({
   898	    connect,
   899	    disconnect,
   900	    getStatus,
   901	    subscribe,
   902	    subscribeMany,
   903	    unsubscribe,
   904	    unsubscribeMany,
   905	    recoverStaleSubscription,
   906	    removeConsumer,
   907	    onMessage,
   908	    onError,
   909	    getDepthSnapshot,
   910	    getOrderBook,
   911	    getOrderBookStatus: getOrderBook,
   912	    getFeedHealth,
   913	    getOperationalStats,
   914	    quiesce,
   915	    fetchRecentKlines,
   916	    fetchExchangeInfo: fetchExchangeInfoGated,
   917	  });
   918	}

   170	  function notifyErrorListeners(payload) {
   171	    for (const listener of [...errorListeners]) safelyInvokeListener(listener, Object.freeze(payload));

   173	
   174	  /** Resolves true after `ms`, or false at once when `signal` aborts first. */
   175	  function sleep(ms, signal) {
   176	    return new Promise((resolve) => {
   177	      if (signal.aborted) {
   178	        resolve(false);
   179	        return;
   180	      }
   181	      const timer = setTimeout(() => {
   182	        signal.removeEventListener("abort", onAbort);
   183	        resolve(true);
   184	      }, Math.max(1, ms));
   185	      timer.unref?.();
   186	      function onAbort() {
   187	        clearTimeout(timer);
   188	        resolve(false);
   189	      }
   190	      signal.addEventListener("abort", onAbort, { once: true });
   191	    });
   192	  }
   193	
   194	  // The dispatch gate every request of this transport passes: it waits out a
   195	  // ban or rate-limit pause, then reserves the request's weight, waiting for
   196	  // the next minute when the budget is spent. False means the wait was aborted.
   197	  async function awaitDispatch(weight, signal) {
   198	    for (;;) {
   199	      if (signal.aborted) return false;
   200	      const wait = waitBeforeDispatchMs();
   201	      if (wait > 0) {
   202	        if (!(await sleep(wait, signal))) return false;
   203	        continue;
   204	      }
   205	      if (budget.reserve(weight)) return true;
   206	      counters.budgetDeferrals += 1;
   207	      if (!(await sleep(budget.msUntilReset(), signal))) return false;
   208	    }
   209	  }
   210	
   211	  // A request issued on behalf of another module (the candle backfill, the
   212	  // tick-size lookup): gated, weighed, its headers read back, its failure fed
   213	  // to the shared rate-limit, ban and route handling, and abortable by the
   214	  // caller's signal or by disconnect().
   215	  async function gatedRequest(weight, externalSignal, perform) {
   216	    if (externalSignal !== undefined && !(externalSignal instanceof AbortSignal)) {
   217	      throw new Error("signal must be an AbortSignal when provided");
   218	    }
   219	    const controller = new AbortController();
   220	    const forwardAbort = () => controller.abort();
   221	    if (externalSignal?.aborted) controller.abort();
   222	    else externalSignal?.addEventListener("abort", forwardAbort, { once: true });
   223	    gatedControllers.add(controller);
   224	    try {
   225	      if (!(await awaitDispatch(weight, controller.signal))) return Object.freeze({ ok: false, reason: "ABORTED" });
   226	      let result;
   227	      try {
   228	        result = await perform(controller.signal);
   229	      } catch (error) {
   230	        result = Object.freeze({ ok: false, reason: error?.name === "AbortError" ? "ABORTED" : "NETWORK_ERROR" });
   231	      }
   232	      observeWeight(result);
   233	      if (!result.ok && result.reason !== "ABORTED") noteRequestFailure(result);
   234	      return result;
   235	    } finally {
   236	      gatedControllers.delete(controller);
   237	      externalSignal?.removeEventListener("abort", forwardAbort);
   238	    }
   239	  }
   240	
   241	  function gatedFetchKlines(marketKind, symbol, options = {}) {
   242	    return gatedRequest(REQUEST_WEIGHTS.KLINES, options.signal, (signal) =>
   243	      fetchKlines(marketKind, symbol, { ...options, fetchImpl, timeoutMs: requestTimeoutMs, signal }));
   244	  }

   247	  function fetchExchangeInfoGated(marketKind, symbol, options = {}) {

   249	      fetchExchangeInfo(marketKind, symbol, {
   250	        ...options,
   251	        fetchImpl,

   253	        timeoutMs: requestTimeoutMs,
   254	        signal,
   255	      }));
   256	  }

   258	  function setRouteState(next, fields = {}) {
   259	    if (routeState === next) return;
   260	    routeState = next;
   261	    if (next === "CONNECTED") feedHealth.recordRouteConnected(MARKET_KINDS.SPOT, ROUTE_NAME);
   262	    else feedHealth.recordRouteDisconnected(MARKET_KINDS.SPOT, ROUTE_NAME);
   263	    telemetryEvent(next === "CONNECTED" ? "route_connected" : "route_disconnected", { state: next, ...fields });
   264	  }
   265	
   266	  function backoffDelayMs(attempt) {
   267	    const raw = Math.min(BACKOFF_BASE_DELAY_MS * 2 ** Math.max(0, attempt - 1), BACKOFF_MAX_DELAY_MS);
   268	    const jitter = raw * BACKOFF_JITTER_RATIO * (random() * 2 - 1);
   269	    return Math.max(0, Math.round(raw + jitter));
   270	  }
   271	
   272	  function clearTimer(holder) {
   273	    if (holder.timer) clearTimeout(holder.timer);
   274	    holder.timer = null;
   275	  }
   276	
   277	  function schedule(holder, delayMs, run) {
   278	    clearTimer(holder);
   279	    const generationAtSchedule = sessionGeneration;
   280	    holder.timer = setTimeout(() => {
   281	      holder.timer = null;
   282	      if (!accepting || generationAtSchedule !== sessionGeneration) return;
   283	      run().catch((error) => {
   284	        notifyErrorListeners({ marketKind: MARKET_KINDS.SPOT, route: ROUTE_NAME, message: error?.message ?? String(error), code: "REST_INTERNAL" });
   285	      });
   286	    }, Math.max(0, delayMs));
   287	    holder.timer.unref?.();
   288	  }
   289	
   290	  function waitBeforeDispatchMs() {
   291	    const at = now();
   292	    return Math.max(0, banUntilMs - at, rateLimitedUntilMs - at);
   293	  }
   294	
   295	  function requestOptions(signal) {
   296	    return { fetchImpl, baseUrl: spotBaseUrl, timeoutMs: requestTimeoutMs, signal };
   297	  }
   298	
   299	  function observeWeight(result) {
   300	    const headers = result.ok ? result.value.headers : result.headers;
   301	    if (headers) budget.observe(headers);
   302	  }
   303	
   304	  function applyServerTime(serverTime, sentAt, receivedAt) {
   305	    exchangeOffsetMs = serverTime - Math.round((sentAt + receivedAt) / 2);
   306	    clockSyncedAtMs = receivedAt;
   307	    counters.clockSyncs += 1;
   308	  }
   309	
   310	  // The route is up once exchangeInfo (weight 20) answers: it proves the
   311	  // origin, the proxy and the symbol, and its serverTime seeds the exchange
   312	  // clock every candle is classified against.
   313	  async function runProbe() {
   314	    if (!accepting || probe.inflight) return;
   315	    const wait = waitBeforeDispatchMs();
   316	    if (wait > 0) {
   317	      schedule(probe, wait, runProbe);
   318	      return;
   319	    }
   320	    if (!budget.reserve(REQUEST_WEIGHTS.EXCHANGE_INFO)) {
   321	      counters.budgetDeferrals += 1;
   322	      schedule(probe, budget.msUntilReset(), runProbe);
   323	      return;
   324	    }
   325	    const controller = new AbortController();
   326	    probe.inflight = controller;
   327	    const generation = sessionGeneration;
   328	    const sentAt = now();
   329	    counters.probes += 1;
   330	    let result;
   331	    try {
   332	      result = await fetchExchangeInfo(MARKET_KINDS.SPOT, probeSymbol, requestOptions(controller.signal));
   333	    } catch {
   334	      result = Object.freeze({ ok: false, reason: "NETWORK_ERROR" });
   335	    }
   336	    probe.inflight = null;
   337	    if (!accepting || generation !== sessionGeneration) return;
   338	    const receivedAt = now();
   339	    observeWeight(result);
   340	    const serverTime = result.ok ? result.value.body?.serverTime : undefined;
   341	    if (!result.ok || !Number.isSafeInteger(serverTime)) {
   342	      probe.attempt += 1;
   343	      telemetryEvent("probe_failed", { reason: result.ok ? "MALFORMED_RESPONSE_BODY" : result.reason, attempt: probe.attempt });
   344	      if (!result.ok) noteRequestFailure(result);
   345	      schedule(probe, Math.max(waitBeforeDispatchMs(), backoffDelayMs(probe.attempt)), runProbe);
   346	      return;
   347	    }
   348	    probe.attempt = 0;
   349	    applyServerTime(serverTime, sentAt, receivedAt);
   350	    consecutiveTransportFailures = 0;
   351	    setRouteState("CONNECTED");
   352	    scheduleClockRefresh();
   353	    for (const poller of pollers.values()) schedulePoll(poller, 0);
   354	  }
   355	
   356	  // The exchange clock is re-read from /api/v3/time (weight 1, reserved like
   357	  // every request) once a minute; a failed refresh keeps the last offset.
   358	  function scheduleClockRefresh() {
   359	    schedule(clock, clockRefreshMs, refreshClock);
   360	  }
   361	
   362	  async function refreshClock() {
   363	    if (!accepting || routeState !== "CONNECTED" || clock.inflight) return;
   364	    if (waitBeforeDispatchMs() > 0 || !budget.reserve(REQUEST_WEIGHTS.SERVER_TIME)) {
   365	      counters.budgetDeferrals += 1;
   366	      scheduleClockRefresh();
   367	      return;
   368	    }
   369	    const controller = new AbortController();
   370	    clock.inflight = controller;
   371	    const generation = sessionGeneration;
   372	    const sentAt = now();
   373	    let result;
   374	    try {
   375	      result = await fetchServerTime(MARKET_KINDS.SPOT, requestOptions(controller.signal));
   376	    } catch {
   377	      result = Object.freeze({ ok: false, reason: "NETWORK_ERROR" });
   378	    }
   379	    clock.inflight = null;
   380	    if (!accepting || generation !== sessionGeneration) return;
   381	    observeWeight(result);
   382	    if (result.ok && Number.isSafeInteger(result.value.body?.serverTime)) {
   383	      applyServerTime(result.value.body.serverTime, sentAt, now());
   384	    } else {
   385	      // The last valid offset stays; the failure itself gets the shared
   386	      // handling, so a 429 pauses polling and a 418 opens the ban window.
   387	      counters.clockRefreshFailures += 1;
   388	      telemetryEvent("clock_refresh_failed", { reason: result.ok ? "MALFORMED_RESPONSE_BODY" : result.reason, status: result.status ?? null });
   389	      if (!result.ok && result.reason !== "ABORTED") noteRequestFailure(result);
   390	    }
   391	    scheduleClockRefresh();
   392	  }
   393	
   394	  // Bookkeeping shared by every failed request: 429 pauses everything until
   395	  // Retry-After (or the backoff); 418 is an IP ban that stops polling for the
   396	  // whole window; three transport failures in a row mean the route is down.
   397	  function noteRequestFailure(result) {
   398	    counters.pollFailures += 1;
   399	    const at = now();
   400	    if (result.reason === "IP_BANNED") {
   401	      banUntilMs = Math.max(banUntilMs, at + (result.retryAfterMs ?? defaultBanMs));
   402	      telemetryEvent("ip_banned", { untilMs: banUntilMs, retryAfterMs: result.retryAfterMs ?? null });
   403	      notifyErrorListeners({ marketKind: MARKET_KINDS.SPOT, route: ROUTE_NAME, message: "Binance banned this IP; polling stops for the Retry-After window.", code: 418 });
   404	      setRouteState("DISCONNECTED", { reason: "IP_BANNED" });
   405	      schedule(probe, banUntilMs - at, runProbe);
   406	      return;
   407	    }
   408	    if (result.reason === "RATE_LIMITED") {
   409	      rateLimitedUntilMs = Math.max(rateLimitedUntilMs, at + (result.retryAfterMs ?? backoffDelayMs(1)));
   410	      telemetryEvent("rate_limited", { untilMs: rateLimitedUntilMs, retryAfterMs: result.retryAfterMs ?? null });
   411	      return;
   412	    }
   413	    if (TRANSPORT_FAILURE_REASONS.has(result.reason)) {
   414	      consecutiveTransportFailures += 1;
   415	      if (routeState === "CONNECTED" && consecutiveTransportFailures >= CONSECUTIVE_FAILURES_BEFORE_DISCONNECT) {
   416	        notifyErrorListeners({ marketKind: MARKET_KINDS.SPOT, route: ROUTE_NAME, message: "Binance REST polling failed repeatedly; the route is down until a probe succeeds." });
   417	        setRouteState("DISCONNECTED", { reason: result.reason });
   418	        schedule(probe, backoffDelayMs(1), runProbe);
   419	      }
   420	    }
   421	  }
   422	
   423	  function replan() {
   424	    plan = planCadence({ klinePollers: pollers.size, pollMs, budgetPerMinute: weightBudgetPerMinute });
   425	  }
   426	
   427	  function createPoller(descriptor) {
   428	    return {
   429	      descriptor,
   430	      key: pollerKey(descriptor),
   431	      timer: null,
   432	      inflight: null,
   433	      epoch: 0,
   434	      seq: 0,
   435	      attempt: 0,
   436	      catchUpPages: 0,
   437	      catchingUp: false,
   438	      lastFailureReason: null,
   439	      lastLatencyMs: null,
   440	      lastSuccessAtMs: null,
   441	      lastSentAtMs: null,
   442	      budgetDeferred: false,
   443	      bars: createBinanceRestBarCursor({ intervalMs: candleIntervalMsFor(descriptor.interval), finalizeGraceMs }),
   444	    };
   445	  }
   446	
   447	  function resetPollerState(poller) {
   448	    poller.bars.reset();
   449	    poller.catchUpPages = 0;
   450	    poller.catchingUp = false;
   451	    poller.attempt = 0;
   452	  }
   453	
   454	  function schedulePoll(poller, delayMs) {
   455	    schedule(poller, delayMs, () => runPoll(poller));
   456	  }
   457	
   458	  function scheduleNextPoll(poller, { immediate = false } = {}) {
   459	    if (immediate) {
   460	      schedulePoll(poller, 0);
   461	      return;
   462	    }
   463	    const elapsed = poller.lastSentAtMs === null ? 0 : now() - poller.lastSentAtMs;
   464	    schedulePoll(poller, Math.max(0, plan.klineCadenceMs - elapsed));
   465	  }
   466	
   467	  function beginRecovery(poller) {
   468	    const { marketKind, symbol, eventType, interval } = poller.descriptor;
   469	    feedHealth.beginRecovery(marketKind, symbol, eventType, interval);
   470	  }
   471	
   472	  function endRecovery(poller) {
   473	    const { marketKind, symbol, eventType, interval } = poller.descriptor;
   474	    feedHealth.endRecovery(marketKind, symbol, eventType, interval);
   475	  }
   476	
   477	  async function runPoll(poller) {
   478	    if (!accepting || routeState !== "CONNECTED" || poller.inflight || pollers.get(poller.key) !== poller) return;
   479	    const wait = waitBeforeDispatchMs();
   480	    if (wait > 0) {
   481	      schedulePoll(poller, wait);
   482	      return;
   483	    }
   484	    // Weight is reserved before dispatch; a refusal waits for the next minute.
   485	    if (!budget.reserve(REQUEST_WEIGHTS.KLINES)) {
   486	      counters.budgetDeferrals += 1;
   487	      poller.budgetDeferred = true;
   488	      telemetryEvent("poll_deferred_by_budget", { symbol: safeTelemetrySymbol(poller.descriptor.symbol) });
   489	      schedulePoll(poller, budget.msUntilReset());
   490	      return;
   491	    }
   492	    poller.budgetDeferred = false;
   493	    const controller = new AbortController();
   494	    const epochAtRequest = poller.epoch;
   495	    const generationAtRequest = sessionGeneration;
   496	    const seq = ++poller.seq;
   497	    poller.inflight = controller;
   498	    poller.lastSentAtMs = now();
   499	    counters.polls += 1;
   500	    const request = poller.bars.nextRequest(exchangeNow());
   501	    let result;
   502	    try {
   503	      result = await fetchKlines(MARKET_KINDS.SPOT, poller.descriptor.symbol, {
   504	        ...requestOptions(controller.signal),
   505	        interval: poller.descriptor.interval,
   506	        startTime: request.startTime,
   507	        limit: request.limit,
   508	      });
   509	    } catch (error) {
   510	      result = Object.freeze({ ok: false, reason: error?.name === "AbortError" ? "ABORTED" : "NETWORK_ERROR" });
   511	    }
   512	    if (poller.inflight === controller) poller.inflight = null;
   513	    // A response for a subscription that ended, or from a session that was

+/** Resolves true after `ms`, or false at once when `signal` aborts first. */
+function delay(ms, signal) {
+  return new Promise((resolve) => {
+    if (signal?.aborted) {
+      resolve(false);
+      return;
+    }
+    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
+      signal?.removeEventListener("abort", onAbort);
+      resolve(true);
+    }, ms);
+    function onAbort() {
+      clearTimeout(timer);
+      resolve(false);
+    }
+    signal?.addEventListener("abort", onAbort, { once: true });
+  });

const T0 = 1_800_000_000_000 - (1_800_000_000_000 % MINUTE);

function rawRow(openTime, { open = "64200.00", high = "64300.50", low = "64150.25", close = "64275.99", volume = "12.3", trades = 418, takerBuy = "6.0" } = {}) {
  return [openTime, open, high, low, close, volume, openTime + MINUTE - 1, "792345.11", trades, takerBuy, "385000.0", "0"];
}

function rows(...raws) {
  const parsed = parseKlineRows(raws, MINUTE);
  if (parsed === null) throw new Error("test rows must parse");
  return parsed;
}

describe("binance-rest-bar-cursor", () => {
  test("classifies every row by its own close time against the exchange clock, not by position: the previous bar is final, the current forms", () => {
    const cursor = createBinanceRestBarCursor({ intervalMs: MINUTE, finalizeGraceMs: 1000 });
    const exchangeNow = T0 + 2 * MINUTE + 30_000;
    expect(cursor.nextRequest(exchangeNow)).toEqual({ startTime: T0 + MINUTE, limit: 2, catchUp: false });
    const outcome = cursor.apply({ rows: rows(rawRow(T0 + MINUTE), rawRow(T0 + 2 * MINUTE)), exchangeNowMs: exchangeNow, seq: 1 });
    expect(outcome.stale).toBe(false);
    expect(outcome.emit.map(({ row, closed }) => [row.openTime - T0, closed])).toEqual([[MINUTE, true], [2 * MINUTE, false]]);
    expect(cursor.confirmedOpenTime).toBe(T0 + MINUTE);
  });

  test("a revision replaces the same forming bar only when it changed; finalisation happens once, with the grace after the close", () => {
    const cursor = createBinanceRestBarCursor({ intervalMs: MINUTE, finalizeGraceMs: 1000 });
    cursor.apply({ rows: rows(rawRow(T0), rawRow(T0 + MINUTE)), exchangeNowMs: T0 + MINUTE + 10_000, seq: 1 });
    const unchanged = cursor.apply({ rows: rows(rawRow(T0), rawRow(T0 + MINUTE)), exchangeNowMs: T0 + MINUTE + 11_000, seq: 2 });
    expect(unchanged.emit).toEqual([]);
    const revised = cursor.apply({ rows: rows(rawRow(T0), rawRow(T0 + MINUTE, { close: "64280.00" })), exchangeNowMs: T0 + MINUTE + 12_000, seq: 3 });
    expect(revised.emit.map(({ row, closed }) => [row.openTime - T0, closed, row.close])).toEqual([[MINUTE, false, "64280.00"]]);
    // Exactly at the close the bar is still forming (the grace has not passed); one second later it is final, once.
    const atClose = cursor.apply({ rows: rows(rawRow(T0 + MINUTE, { close: "64281.00" }), rawRow(T0 + 2 * MINUTE)), exchangeNowMs: T0 + 2 * MINUTE + 500, seq: 4 });
    expect(atClose.emit.map(({ row, closed }) => [row.openTime - T0, closed])).toEqual([[MINUTE, false], [2 * MINUTE, false]]);
    const afterGrace = cursor.apply({ rows: rows(rawRow(T0 + MINUTE, { close: "64281.00" }), rawRow(T0 + 2 * MINUTE)), exchangeNowMs: T0 + 2 * MINUTE + 1000, seq: 5 });
    expect(afterGrace.emit.map(({ row, closed }) => [row.openTime - T0, closed])).toEqual([[MINUTE, true]]);
    const again = cursor.apply({ rows: rows(rawRow(T0 + MINUTE, { close: "64281.00" }), rawRow(T0 + 2 * MINUTE)), exchangeNowMs: T0 + 2 * MINUTE + 2000, seq: 6 });
    expect(again.emit).toEqual([]);
    expect(cursor.confirmedOpenTime).toBe(T0 + MINUTE);
  });

  test("the forming-bar change detector compares every downstream field: open, high, low, close, volume, taker-buy volume and trade count", () => {
    const cursor = createBinanceRestBarCursor({ intervalMs: MINUTE });
    const at = T0 + MINUTE + 10_000;
    let seq = 1;
    const emitForming = (overrides) => cursor.apply({ rows: rows(rawRow(T0), rawRow(T0 + MINUTE, overrides)), exchangeNowMs: at, seq: seq++ }).emit
      .filter(({ closed }) => !closed).length;
    expect(emitForming({})).toBe(1);
    expect(emitForming({})).toBe(0);
    for (const change of [
      { open: "64201.00" },
      { high: "64301.00", open: "64201.00" },
      { low: "64100.00", high: "64301.00", open: "64201.00" },
      { close: "64280.00", low: "64100.00", high: "64301.00", open: "64201.00" },
      { volume: "12.4", close: "64280.00", low: "64100.00", high: "64301.00", open: "64201.00" },
      { takerBuy: "6.1", volume: "12.4", close: "64280.00", low: "64100.00", high: "64301.00", open: "64201.00" },
      { trades: 419, takerBuy: "6.1", volume: "12.4", close: "64280.00", low: "64100.00", high: "64301.00", open: "64201.00" },
    ]) {
      expect(emitForming(change)).toBe(1);
      expect(emitForming(change)).toBe(0);
    }
  });

  test("a response from an older poll never overwrites a newer revision", () => {
    const cursor = createBinanceRestBarCursor({ intervalMs: MINUTE });
    cursor.apply({ rows: rows(rawRow(T0), rawRow(T0 + MINUTE, { close: "64280.00" })), exchangeNowMs: T0 + MINUTE + 5000, seq: 2 });
    const late = cursor.apply({ rows: rows(rawRow(T0), rawRow(T0 + MINUTE, { close: "64270.00" })), exchangeNowMs: T0 + MINUTE + 6000, seq: 1 });
    expect(late.stale).toBe(true);
    expect(late.emit).toEqual([]);
    const next = cursor.apply({ rows: rows(rawRow(T0), rawRow(T0 + MINUTE, { close: "64280.00" })), exchangeNowMs: T0 + MINUTE + 7000, seq: 3 });
    expect(next.emit).toEqual([]);
  });

  test("clock skew: a machine clock ahead of the exchange cannot finalise a bar the exchange still has open", () => {
    const cursor = createBinanceRestBarCursor({ intervalMs: MINUTE, finalizeGraceMs: 1000 });
    const machineNow = T0 + 2 * MINUTE + 20_000;
    const exchangeNow = machineNow - 30_000; // the machine runs 30 s ahead
    expect(cursor.nextRequest(exchangeNow)).toEqual({ startTime: T0, limit: 2, catchUp: false });
    const outcome = cursor.apply({ rows: rows(rawRow(T0), rawRow(T0 + MINUTE)), exchangeNowMs: exchangeNow, seq: 1 });
    // By the machine clock the T0+1m bar closed 20 s ago; by the exchange it has 10 s to run.
    expect(outcome.emit.map(({ row, closed }) => [row.openTime - T0, closed])).toEqual([[0, true], [MINUTE, false]]);
  });

  test("after a multi-minute outage it catches up from the last confirmed close in bounded pages before the two-row poll resumes", () => {
    const cursor = createBinanceRestBarCursor({ intervalMs: MINUTE });
    cursor.apply({ rows: rows(rawRow(T0), rawRow(T0 + MINUTE)), exchangeNowMs: T0 + MINUTE + 5000, seq: 1 });
    expect(cursor.confirmedOpenTime).toBe(T0);
    const afterOutage = T0 + 6 * MINUTE + 5000;
    expect(cursor.needsCatchUp(afterOutage)).toBe(true);
    expect(cursor.nextRequest(afterOutage)).toEqual({ startTime: T0 + MINUTE, limit: 1000, catchUp: true });
    const page = cursor.apply({
      rows: rows(rawRow(T0 + MINUTE), rawRow(T0 + 2 * MINUTE), rawRow(T0 + 3 * MINUTE), rawRow(T0 + 4 * MINUTE), rawRow(T0 + 5 * MINUTE), rawRow(T0 + 6 * MINUTE)),
      exchangeNowMs: afterOutage,
      seq: 2,
    });
    expect(page.emit.map(({ row, closed }) => [row.openTime / MINUTE - T0 / MINUTE, closed])).toEqual([[1, true], [2, true], [3, true], [4, true], [5, true], [6, false]]);
    expect(page.hole).toBe(false);
    expect(page.catchingUp).toBe(false);
    expect(cursor.confirmedOpenTime).toBe(T0 + 5 * MINUTE);
    expect(cursor.nextRequest(afterOutage)).toEqual({ startTime: T0 + 5 * MINUTE, limit: 2, catchUp: false });
    // A page that stops short keeps the catch-up going.
    const stillBehind = createBinanceRestBarCursor({ intervalMs: MINUTE });
    stillBehind.apply({ rows: rows(rawRow(T0)), exchangeNowMs: T0 + MINUTE + 5000, seq: 1 });
    const partial = stillBehind.apply({ rows: rows(rawRow(T0 + MINUTE), rawRow(T0 + 2 * MINUTE)), exchangeNowMs: T0 + 10 * MINUTE, seq: 2 });
    expect(partial.catchingUp).toBe(true);
    expect(stillBehind.nextRequest(T0 + 10 * MINUTE)).toEqual({ startTime: T0 + 3 * MINUTE, limit: 1000, catchUp: true });
  });

  test("a hole the exchange left is reported, never filled, and the cursor moves past it", () => {
    const cursor = createBinanceRestBarCursor({ intervalMs: MINUTE });
    cursor.apply({ rows: rows(rawRow(T0)), exchangeNowMs: T0 + 10 * MINUTE, seq: 1 });
    const outcome = cursor.apply({ rows: rows(rawRow(T0 + 3 * MINUTE), rawRow(T0 + 4 * MINUTE)), exchangeNowMs: T0 + 10 * MINUTE, seq: 2 });
    expect(outcome.hole).toBe(true);
    expect(outcome.emit).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(cursor.stats).toMatchObject({ confirmedOpenTime: T0 + 4 * MINUTE, holes: 1 });
    cursor.reset();
    expect(cursor.confirmedOpenTime).toBeNull();
    expect(() => createBinanceRestBarCursor({ intervalMs: 0 })).toThrow(/intervalMs/);
  });
});

412:function isValidKlineTimeRange(openTime, closeTime, interval) {
416:    openTime % intervalMs === 0 &&

  function broadcastMarkerHistoryIncrement(
    pairSymbol,
    pairMarketKind,
    markers,
  ) {
    if (markers.length === 0) return;
    const key = pairKey(pairSymbol, pairMarketKind);
    const identity = {
      provider: "BINANCE",
      symbol: pairSymbol,
      marketKind: pairMarketKind,
    };
    const ordered = [...markers].sort(
      (a, b) =>
        a.ts_exchange - b.ts_exchange ||
        (a.trade_id < b.trade_id ? -1 : a.trade_id > b.trade_id ? 1 : 0),
    );
    let seq = markerHistorySeqByPair.get(key) ?? 0;
    for (
      let start = 0;
      start < ordered.length;
      start += MAX_MARKER_HISTORY_ROWS
    ) {

      seq += 1;
    }

  }

    'function delay(ms, signal) {\n' +
    '  return new Promise((resolve) => {\n' +
    '    if (signal?.aborted) {\n' +
    '      resolve(false);\n' +
    '      return;\n' +
    '    }\n' +
    '    const timer = setTimeout(() => {\n' +
    '      signal?.removeEventListener("abort", onAbort);\n' +
    '      resolve(true);\n' +
    '    }, ms);\n' +
    '    function onAbort() {\n' +
    '      clearTimeout(timer);\n' +
    '      resolve(false);\n' +
    '    }\n' +
    '    signal?.addEventListener("abort", onAbort, { once: true });\n' +
    '  });\n' +
    '}\n' +
    '\n' +
    '/**\n' +
    ' * `now` is the clock candles are classified against and `finalizeGraceMs` the\n' +
    " * time after a candle's close before it counts as final: the stream transport\n" +
    ' * uses the machine clock with no grace, the REST transport its synchronised\n' +
    ' * exchange clock with the same grace as its live polling, so a candle the\n' +
    ' * exchange still has open is never stored as closed history.\n' +
    ' */\n' +
    'function createBinanceKlineBackfill({\n' +
    '  fetchImpl = globalThis.fetch,\n' +
    '  fetchKlinesImpl = fetchKlines,\n' +
    '  telemetry,\n' +
    '  endpoints = DEFAULT_BINANCE_ENDPOINTS,\n' +
    '  now = () => Date.now(),\n' +
    '  finalizeGraceMs = 0,\n' +
    '} = {}) {\n' +
    '  if (typeof fetchKlinesImpl !== "function") throw new Error("createBinanceKlineBackfill: fetchKlinesImpl must be a function");\n' +
    '  if (typeof now !== "function") throw new Error("createBinanceKlineBackfill: now must be a function");\n' +
    '  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(finalizeGraceMs) || finalizeGraceMs < 0) {\n' +
    '    throw new Error("createBinanceKlineBackfill: finalizeGraceMs must be a non-negative safe integer");\n' +
    '  }\n' +
    '\n' +
    '  async function fetchRecentBars(\n' +
    '    marketKind,\n' +
    '    symbol,\n' +
    '    { days = DEFAULT_DAYS, interval, intervalMs, pageDelayMs = DEFAULT_PAGE_DELAY_MS, maxPages = DEFAULT_MAX_PAGES, signal } = {},\n' +
    '  ) {\n' +
    '    assertAllowedMarketKind(marketKind);\n' +
    '    assertAllowedSymbol(symbol);\n' +
    '    if (!isAllowedCandleInterval(interval)) {\n' +
    '      throw new Error(`fetchRecentBars: unrecognized interval "${interval}"`);\n' +
    '    }\n' +
    '    if (!Number.isInteger(intervalMs) || intervalMs <= 0) {\n' +
    '      throw new Error("fetchRecentBars: intervalMs must be a positive integer");\n' +
    '    }\n' +
    '    if (intervalMs !== candleIntervalMsFor(interval)) {\n' +
    '      throw new Error("fetchRecentBars: intervalMs must match the requested interval");\n' +
    '    }\n' +
    '    if (!Number.isFinite(days) || days <= 0) {\n' +
    '      throw new Error("fetchRecentBars: days must be a positive number");\n' +
    '    }\n' +
    '    if (!Number.isInteger(pageDelayMs) || pageDelayMs < 0) {\n' +
    '      throw new Error("fetchRecentBars: pageDelayMs must be a non-negative integer");\n' +
    '    }\n' +
    '    if (!Number.isInteger(maxPages) || maxPages <= 0 || maxPages > MAX_BACKFILL_PAGES) {\n' +
    '      throw new Error(`fetchRecentBars: maxPages must be a positive integer <= ${MAX_BACKFILL_PAGES}`);\n' +
    '    }\n' +
    '    if (signal !== undefined && !(signal instanceof AbortSignal)) {\n' +
    '      throw new Error("fetchRecentBars: signal must be an AbortSignal when provided");\n' +
    '    }\n' +
    '\n' +
    '    const rows = [];\n' +
    '    let retryAfterMs;\n' +
    '    const startedAtMs = now();\n' +
    '    // The first open time that is not final: a candle is final once its close\n' +
    '    // time plus the grace has passed on the clock given to this backfill.\n' +
    '    const closedBoundaryAt = (atMs) => Math.floor((atMs + 1 - finalizeGraceMs) / intervalMs) * intervalMs;\n' +
    '    const lookbackMs = days * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;\n' +
    '    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(lookbackMs) || lookbackMs > startedAtMs) {\n' +
    '      throw new Error("fetchRecentBars: days resolves outside the supported epoch range");\n' +
    '    }\n' +
    '    let cursor = closedBoundaryAt(startedAtMs - lookbackMs);\n' +
    '    let pagesFetched = 0;\n' +
    '    let failureReason = null;\n' +
    '    let caughtUp = false;\n' +
    '\n' +
    '    while (pagesFetched < maxPages) {\n' +
    '      if (signal?.aborted) {\n' +
    '        failureReason = "ABORTED";\n' +
    '        break;\n' +
    '      }\n' +
    '      const closedBoundary = closedBoundaryAt(now());\n' +
    '      if (cursor >= closedBoundary) {\n' +
    '        caughtUp = true;\n' +
    '        break;\n' +
    '      }\n' +
    '\n' +
    '      const result = await fetchKlinesImpl(marketKind, symbol, {\n' +
    '        interval,\n' +
    '        startTime: cursor,\n' +
    '        limit: MAX_KLINES_PER_REQUEST,\n' +
    '        fetchImpl,\n' +
    '        baseUrl: restBaseUrlFor(marketKind, endpoints),\n' +
    '        signal,\n' +
    '      });\n' +
    '      pagesFetched += 1;\n' +
    '\n' +
    '      if (!result.ok) {\n' +
    '        failureReason = result.reason;\n' +
    '        if (Number.isSafeInteger(result.retryAfterMs) && result.retryAfterMs >= 0) {\n' +
    '          retryAfterMs = result.retryAfterMs;\n' +
    '        }\n' +
    '        emitTelemetry(telemetry, "market_data.binance_kline_backfill.page_failed", {\n' +
    '          symbol: safeTelemetrySymbol(symbol),\n' +
    '          marketKind: safeTelemetryMarketKind(marketKind),\n' +
    '          reason: result.reason,\n' +
    '          pagesFetched,\n' +
    '          barsSoFar: rows.length,\n' +
    '        });\n' +
    '        break;\n' +
    '      }\n' +
    '\n' +
    '      const page = parseKlineRows(result.value.body, intervalMs);\n' +
    '      if (!page) {\n' +
    '        failureReason = "MALFORMED_KLINE_PAGE";\n' +
    '        emitTelemetry(telemetry, "market_data.binance_kline_backfill.page_failed", {\n' +
    '          symbol: safeTelemetrySymbol(symbol),\n' +
    '          marketKind: safeTelemetryMarketKind(marketKind),\n' +
    '          reason: failureReason,\n' +
    '          pagesFetched,\n' +
    '          barsSoFar: rows.length,\n' +
    '        });\n' +
    '        break;\n' +
    '      }\n' +
    '\n' +
    '      if (page.length === 0) {\n' +
    '        failureReason = "INCOMPLETE_PAGE";\n' +
    '        break;\n' +
    '      }\n' +
    '\n' +
    '      if (page[0].openTime !== cursor) {\n' +
    '        failureReason = "PAGE_START_MISMATCH";\n' +
    '        break;\n' +
    '      }\n' +
    '      if (page[page.length - 1].openTime > closedBoundary) {\n' +
    '        failureReason = "PAGE_TIME_RANGE_INVALID";\n' +
    '        break;\n' +
    '      }\n' +
    '\n' +
    '      const closedPage = page[page.length - 1].openTime === closedBoundary\n' +
    '        ? page.slice(0, -1)\n' +
    '        : page;\n' +
    '      if (closedPage.length === 0) {\n' +
    '        failureReason = "INCOMPLETE_PAGE";\n' +
    '        break;\n' +
    '      }\n' +
    '\n' +
    '      for (const row of closedPage) {\n' +
    '        rows.push(row);\n' +
    '      }\n' +
    '\n' +
    '      const lastRow = closedPage[closedPage.length - 1];\n' +
    '      const nextCursor = lastRow.openTime + intervalMs;\n' +
    '      if (nextCursor <= cursor) {\n' +
    '        failureReason = "NON_ADVANCING_PAGE";\n' +
    '        break;\n' +
    '      }\n' +
    '      cursor = nextCursor;\n' +
    '\n' +
    '      if (cursor >= closedBoundary) {\n' +
    '        caughtUp = true;\n' +
    '        break;\n' +
    '      }\n' +
    '      if (page.length < MAX_KLINES_PER_REQUEST) {\n' +
    '        failureReason = "INCOMPLETE_PAGE";\n' +
    '        break;\n' +
    '      }\n' +
    '      if (cursor < closedBoundary && !(await delay(pageDelayMs, signal))) {\n' +
    '        failureReason = "ABORTED";\n' +
    '        break;\n' +
    '      }\n' +
    '    }\n' +
    '\n' +
    '    if (failureReason === null && !caughtUp && cursor < closedBoundaryAt(now())) {\n' +
    '      failureReason = "MAX_PAGES_REACHED";\n' +
    '    }\n' +
    '\n' +
    '    emitTelemetry(telemetry, "market_data.binance_kline_backfill.completed", {\n' +
    '      symbol: safeTelemetrySymbol(symbol),\n' +
    '      marketKind: safeTelemetryMarketKind(marketKind),\n' +
    '      barsFetched: rows.length,\n' +
    '      pagesFetched,\n' +
    '      ok: failureReason === null,\n' +
    '    });\n' +
    '\n' +
    '    return {\n' +
    '      ok: failureReason === null,\n' +
    '      rows: Object.freeze(rows),\n' +
    '      ...(failureReason !== null ? { reason: failureReason } : {}),\n' +
    '      ...(retryAfterMs !== undefined ? { retryAfterMs } : {}),\n' +
    '    };\n' +
    '  }\n' +
    '\n' +
    '  return Object.freeze({ fetchRecentBars });\n' +
    '}\n' +
    '\n' +
    'module.exports = { createBinanceKlineBackfill, DEFAULT_DAYS, DEFAULT_MAX_PAGES };\n',
  stderr: ''
}

test("HTTP helpers reject invalid resource controls before invoking fetch", async () => {
  const fetchImpl = jest.fn();
  await expect(fetchDepthSnapshot("SPOT", "BTCUSDT", { fetchImpl, timeoutMs: 0 })).rejects.toThrow(/timeoutMs/);
  await expect(fetchDepthSnapshot("SPOT", "BTCUSDT", { fetchImpl, maxResponseBytes: Number.NaN })).rejects.toThrow(/maxResponseBytes/);
  expect(fetchImpl).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
});

test("a genuine but already-locked response body fails closed instead of throwing", async () => {
  const response = jsonResponse({ lastUpdateId: 1, bids: [], asks: [] });
  const reader = response.body.getReader();
  try {
    await expect(fetchDepthSnapshot("SPOT", "BTCUSDT", { fetchImpl: async () => response })).resolves.toEqual({
      ok: false,
      reason: "INVALID_RESPONSE",
    });
  } finally {
    reader.releaseLock();
  }
});

  const response = jsonResponse({ lastUpdateId: 1, bids: [], asks: [] });
  Object.defineProperty(response, "headers", {
    configurable: true,
    get() {
      throw new Error("hostile getter");
    },

  await expect(fetchDepthSnapshot("SPOT", "BTCUSDT", { fetchImpl: async () => response })).resolves.toEqual({

  });

describe("binance-http-client fetchServerTime and cancellation", () => {
  test("fetchServerTime asks /api/v3/time (or /fapi/v1/time) and returns the body with its headers", async () => {
    const fetchImpl = jest.fn(async () => jsonResponse({ serverTime: 1_800_000_000_000 }, { headers: { "x-mbx-used-weight-1m": "3" } }));
    const result = await fetchServerTime("SPOT", { fetchImpl, baseUrl: "https://data-api.binance.vision" });
    await fetchServerTime("PERPETUAL", { fetchImpl });
    expect(fetchImpl.mock.calls.map((call) => call[0])).toEqual([
      "https://data-api.binance.vision/api/v3/time",
      "https://fapi.binance.com/fapi/v1/time",
    ]);
    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
    expect(result.value.body).toEqual({ serverTime: 1_800_000_000_000 });
    expect(result.value.headers["x-mbx-used-weight-1m"]).toBe("3");
    await expect(fetchServerTime("COIN_M", { fetchImpl })).rejects.toThrow(/unrecognized/);
  });

  test("an external AbortSignal cancels the request and classifies it ABORTED, never TIMEOUT or NETWORK_ERROR", async () => {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const fetchImpl = jest.fn((url, init) => new Promise((_, reject) => {
      init.signal.addEventListener("abort", () => {
        const error = new Error("aborted");
        error.name = "AbortError";
        reject(error);
      });
    }));
    const pending = fetchKlines("SPOT", "BTCUSDT", { fetchImpl, interval: "1m", startTime: 0, limit: 2, signal: controller.signal });
    controller.abort();
    expect(await pending).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: "ABORTED" });
    const alreadyAborted = await fetchKlines("SPOT", "BTCUSDT", { fetchImpl, interval: "1m", startTime: 0, signal: controller.signal });
    expect(alreadyAborted).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: "ABORTED" });
    expect(fetchImpl).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    await expect(fetchServerTime("SPOT", { fetchImpl, signal: {} })).rejects.toThrow(/AbortSignal/);
  });
});
